> What is the correct sequence of steps for preparing the base emulsion mixture?

- **A**: 1. Add 4.565 milliliters of decane to the beaker
2. Add 2.054 milliliters of deionized water to a 100 milliliter glass beaker
3. Dissolve 0.1 gram of SDS by stirring the solution
4. Add five milligrams of water-soluble medium viscosity CMC sodium salt to the beaker
5. Stir mixture for five minutes or until CMC sodium salt dissolves using plastic/plastic-coated tools
6. Add 0.844 grams of water-insoluble pre-swollen carboxymethyl cellulose resin to the beaker
7. Stir entire mixture at 1,000 RPM for five minutes using an overhead paddle stirrer with plastic paddle
8. Add 50 grams of zinc powder

> What is the correct sequence of steps for the buffer washing experimental procedure?

- **A**: 1. Immerse well maker in tray filled with Buffer 1 preheated at 50°C

> What is the correct sequence of steps for the Sample Preparation and Initial Dispersion experimental procedure?

- **A**: 1. Add 50 grams of air-dried soil sample (sieved to 2 mm) into 250 ml conical polypropylene centrifuge tube
2. Add 50 ml of 1.85 g/cm³ SPT solution to centrifuge tube
3. Record soil mass to at least four significant figures
4. Cap tube tightly
5. Shake tube vigorously by hand for 60 seconds to break non-water-stable aggregates
6. Secure tightly sealed tube to platform shaker
7. Shake tube for 2 hours at 40-120 rpm
